```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

GLOBAL_DEADLINE = 0.0
GLOBAL_STOP = False
class OptTimeout(Exception):
    pass

def optimize_callback(res):
    global GLOBAL_STOP
    if time.monotonic() >= GLOBAL_DEADLINE:
        GLOBAL_STOP = True
        raise OptTimeout
```

```
def run_lbfgs(a0, method, param, maxfun, maxiter, deadline):
    if method=='lse':
        fun = obj_lse
        args = (beta,)
    elif method=='weighted':
        fun = obj_weighted
        args = (w,)
    elif method=='quad': fun=obj_quad args=(target, lam)
    GLOBAL_DEADLINE=deadline
    GLOBAL_STOP=False
    GLOBAL_LAST_X=a0.copy()
    try:
       res=minimize(fun, a0, args=args, method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, jac=True, callback=optimize_callback, options=...)
       a_out=res.x
    except OptTimeout:
       a_out=GLOBAL_LAST_X
    return a_out
```

def filter_objective(x, base, M):
    h=x
    h=np.maximum(h,0)
    s=h.sum()
    if s<=0: return 1e100

    H=np.fft.rfft(h,M)

Algorithm:
```
def gs_square(a, target, n_iter, alpha_init, alpha_final):
  n=len(a); M=next_pow2(2*n-2)
  A=np.fft.fft(a,M)
  T=np.fft.fft(target[:M],M)
  best...
  for it:
    alpha=...
    A_target=np.sqrt(T)
    # choose root
    choice=np.real(A_target*np.conj(A)) <0
    A_target[choice] = -A_target[choice]
    A = A + alpha*(A_target - A)
    a_new=np.fft.ifft(A,M).real[:n]
    a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0); normalize
    score update
  return best
```

Let's carefully implement `fast_conv` and `grad_weightsum`.

```
def fast_conv(a):
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 0: return np.array([])
    L = 2*n - 1
    M = 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, M)[:L]
    # small negative? use np.maximum? max unaffected; for target maybe use max(0)
    return c
```
For gradient `p` length L:
```
def conv_weight_grad(a, p, c=None):
    n=len(a)
    if c is None: c=fast_conv(a)
    L=2*n-1
    out_len = n + L -1  # 3n-2
    M = 1 << (out_len - 1).bit_length()
    ra = a[::-1]
    RA = np.fft.rfft(ra, M)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(RA*P, M)
    g = 2.0 * conv[n-1:2*n-1]  # length n? conv[n-1] index 0 to n-1 inclusive length n
    return c, g
```
Test with n=1: L=1, out_len=1,M=1? `1 << (0).bit_length()`? 0.bit_length()=0 ->1. rfft length1 okay. conv[n-1:2n-1] = conv[0:1] length1. g=2*a0. c=[a0^2]. weighted objective correct.

For LSE p length L. For weighted p normalized. For exact top K p entries maybe.

`smooth_lse_c(c, beta)`:
```
def lse_value_weights(c,beta):
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    z=beta*(c-cmax)
    zmax=float(np.max(z))
    ez=np.exp(z-zmax)
    s=ez.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: p=zeros? fallback
    p=ez/s
    L=cmax+(zmax+np.log(s))/beta
    return L,p
```
If beta huge and all z -inf due overflow? We shift by zmax, zmax<=0? Actually z=beta*(c-cmax) max=0. zmax=0 always if max exactly; due floating, max 0. So ez for max=1. s finite as long as βdiff < ~700; if diff <0, exp underflows to 0, fine. If c has nan, handle. If p sum zero (all underflow impossible because max=1). Good.

Objective LSE:
```
def obj_lse(x, beta):
    a=np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-8:
       GLOBAL_LAST_X=x.copy()
       return 1e100, np.zeros_like(a)
    M? fast_conv
    c=fast_conv(a)
    L,p=lse_value_weights(c,beta)
    _,g=conv_weight_grad(a,p,c)
    score=(2.0*len(a))*L/(S*S)
    grad=(2.0*len(a))*(g*S - 2.0*L)/(S*S*S)
    # cleanup nan
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)): grad=np.zeros_like(a)
    GLOBAL_LAST_X=x.copy()
    return float(score), grad
```
`S*S*S` but formula uses S^3. Since g has units? okay.

For weighted:
```
def obj_weighted(x,w):
   ...
   C=float(np.dot(w,c))
   _,g=conv_weight_grad(a,w,c)
   score=2n*C/S^2
   grad=2n*(g*S - 2*C)/S^3
```
If w includes uniform target? okay.

For active direction refine, need `weighted_grad_only(a,w,c=None)` returns g (C' gradient), not score. Use `conv_weight_grad`.

`softmax_weights(c, beta)` returns p. For top uniform:
```
def top_weights(c, K, uniform_mix=0.0):
   L=len(c)
   idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
   w=np.zeros(L); w[idx]=1.0/K
   if uniform_mix>0: w = (1-uniform_mix)*w + uniform_mix/L
   return w/w.sum()
```

`reweight_loop` pseudo:
```
def reweight_loop(a0, deadline, print_every=10):
  a=norm1(a0); best=score(a); best_a=a.copy()
  n=len(a)
  K_schedule=[1,3,10,30,100,300]
  beta_schedule=[50,500,5000,50000]
  it=0
  while time<deadline-30:
    c=fast_conv(a); update best if score
    if it%2==0:
       K=...
       w=top_weights or softmax
    else:
       beta=...
       w=softmax
    w=0.95w+0.05/L uniform
    # maybe if K=1 and only one max, exact? good
    a=run_lbfgs(a,'weighted',w, maxfun=3000, deadline)
    a=norm1(a)
    it++
    if it%5==0: apply gs_square with adaptive target 50 iters? maybe.
  return best_a
```

Run LSE annealing:
```
def lse_sweep(a0, deadline):
  a=norm1(a0); best...
  betas=[50, 200, 500, 2000, 8000, 30000, 100000, 300000, 1000000]
  for beta in betas:
    if time>deadline break
    t_run=time+ something
    a=run_lbfgs(a,'lse',beta,maxfun=5000, deadline)

GS square root implementation in detail:
`def poly_square_gs(a, target, n_iters=200, alpha_init=0.8, alpha_final=0.05)`.
```
a=norm1(a)
n=len(a); L=2*n-1
M=1 << (L-1).bit_length()
A=np.fft.fft(a,M)
T=np.fft.fft(target, M)  # target length L padded
best=score(a); best_a=a.copy()
for it in range(n_iters):
   alpha=...
   # root
   # T may be zero; sqrt
   A_des=np.sqrt(T)
   # choose branch
   real_part=np.real(A_des * np.conj(A))
   flip=real_part < 0
   A_des[flip] = -A_des[flip]
   # maybe Newton: A_newton=0.5*(A+T/A_safe)
   # A_safe = A + 1e-12*(1+np.abs(A))
   # Use blend between A_des and Newton? Maybe A_des more stable.
   A = A + alpha*(A_des - A)
   a_new=np.fft.ifft(A,M).real[:n]
   a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0)
   S=a_new.sum()
   if S<1e-12: break
   a_new/=S
   sc=score from conv? compute fast to update
   if sc<best: update
return best_a
```
Could A become large or small; scale invariance? A scale changes a scale; we normalize after each. But A in FFT after normalization. Good.

Target choose: For current a, c=norm1(c). `flat=1/L`. `clipped = c.copy(); cap = np.quantile(c, 0.99) or mean + 2std; tcap = np.minimum(c, cap); tcap/=tcap.sum()`. Then `target = (1-mix)*flat + mix*tcap`. Use mix maybe 0.5. For from parent, maybe mix 0.7 current. But if target too current, less improvement. We can try multiple.

Another GS variant: target `t` as `c` but with top peaks reduced to next peak (water-filling). This is more direct: set top K peaks to average of them? E.g., cap at 0.8*max? But then t endpoints? Use normalized. We'll include `adaptive_target(c, mix, quantile)`:
```
def adaptive_target(c, mix=0.5, quantile=0.99):
  c=c/c.sum()
  flat=np.ones(len(c))/len(c)
  cap=np.quantile(c, quantile)
  clipped=np.minimum(c, cap)
  clipped=clipped/clipped.sum()
  return (1-mix)*flat + mix*clipped
```

Let's implement `next_pow2(L)` top-level:
```
def next_power_of_two(L):
    if L <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
```

`norm1(a)`:
```
def normalize_positive(a):
    a=np.maximum(np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64),0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s<1e-12: return np.ones(len(a))/len(a)
    return a/s
```

`score(a)` uses `fast_conv`. For speed, if a normalized, S=1 but compute.

Helper `interpolate_to_length(a, n)`:
```
def interp_to_len(a,n):
   if len(a)==n: return a.copy()
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,len(a)); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   return np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
```

Helper `filter_objective(x, base, base_fft, M)`: for Nelder-Mead.
Need pass base and base_fft. Function:
```
def filter_objective(x, base, base_fft, M):
    h=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
    hs=h.sum()
    if hs<1e-12: return 1e100
    h=h/hs
    H=np.fft.rfft(h,M)
    b=np.fft.irfft(base_fft*H, M)[:len(base)+len(h)-1]
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    S=b.sum()
    if S<1e-12: return 1e100
    b=b/S
    c=fast_conv(b)
    return float(score_from_c(b,c))
```
Can precompute base_fft with M. For M next power of two len(base)+len(h)-1? For convolution base*h length n+m-1. Need zero-padded length `M = next_power_of_two(n + m - 1)`? At least length n+m-1. Use `n + m - 1` not -2. bit_length of L-1. If L= n+m-1, `next_power_of_two(L)` with L-1? Our function takes L desired, returns next power >=L. Use L=n+m-1.
`base_fft=np.fft.rfft(base,M)`. Good.

    best_sc=np.inf; best_b=normalize_positive(base)
    for h0 in inits:
       if time>deadline: break

       # local nelder

       except: h=res.x? catch
       h=...; compute b=apply_filter...
       sc=score; update
    return best_b,best_sc
```

`poly_square_gs` target generation:
```
def flat_target(n):
    L=2*n-1; t=np.ones(L)/L; return t

def adaptive_target(c, mix, quantile):
    c=np.asarray(c,float); cs=c.sum(); if cs<=0: return flat
    cn=c/cs
    cap=np.quantile(cn, quantile) # if quantile=0.99
    clipped=np.minimum(cn, cap)
    clipped_sum=clipped.sum(); if clipped_sum<=0: clipped=cn
    else: clipped=clipped/clipped_sum
    t=(1-mix)*np.ones(len(cn))/len(cn) + mix*clipped
    t=np.maximum(t,0); return t/t.sum()
```
`poly_square_gs(a, target, iters, alpha_init, alpha_final)`:
- To speed scoring, compute conv once if we need target. For update, score.
- `A = np.fft.fft(a, M)`; `T = np.fft.fft(target, M)`.
- target length L, padded.

def run_lbfgs(a0, kind, params, maxfun, maxiter, deadline):
    a0 = normalize_positive(a0)
    n=len(a0)
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)] for ... maybe if n large memory. If n=1? ok.
    if kind=='lse': fun=obj_lse; args=(params[0],)
    elif kind=='weighted': w=params[0]; Pfft...; fun=obj_weighted; args=(w,Pfft,M2,n)
    else? quad? no.
    try:
       res=minimize(fun, a0, args=args, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, callback=lbfgs_callback, bounds=bounds, options=...)
       x=res.x
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): x=a0
    except OptTimeout:
       x=GLOBAL_LAST_X if GLOBAL_LAST_X is not None else a0
    except Exception as e:
       print('LBFGS error', e); x=a0
    return x
```

Let's write robust `lse_value_weights`:
```
def lse_value_weights(c, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if beta <= 1e-12:
        L = float(np.mean(c))
        p = np.full(len(c), 1.0/len(c))
        return L, p
    z = beta * (c - cmax)
    # clip very negative? np.exp handles underflow.
    z = np.where(z < -745, -745, z)  # to avoid all zero? not needed
    ez = np.exp(z)
    s = float(ez.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        # fallback one-hot at argmax
        p=np.zeros(len(c)); p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; return cmax,p
    p=ez/s
    L = cmax + float(np.log(s)) / beta  # since z max 0 after c-cmax? If z not shifted, max=0, log(s) okay. If cmax nan? handle.
    return L, p
```
Here c-cmax max=0, z max=0. No need shift. If beta huge and some z less than -745, exp underflows; s still at least 1. If c has NaN, cmax nan; handle before. `if not np.isfinite(cmax)`: return inf zeros. In objective, c finite. Good.

But if c-cmax has tiny negative? max 0 exactly? float max gives 0. Good.

For beta_eff maybe 1e12, beta*(c-cmax) maybe -1e8; underflow to 0 but max 1. Good.

```
def score_from_c(a,c):
    n=len(a); S=float(a.sum())
    if S<0.01: return np.inf
    maxc=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc<0: maxc=0.0
    return float(2.0*n*maxc/(S*S))
```

def lse_sweep(a0, deadline):
  a=normalize_positive(a0); best=score(a); best_a=a.copy()
  betas=[50,200,1000,5000,25000,100000,400000]
  for beta in betas:
    if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60: break
    sc_before=best
    a_new=run_lbfgs(a,'lse',beta, maxfun=4000 if beta>10000 else 6000, maxiter=2000, deadline=deadline-30)
    a=normalize_positive(a_new); sc=score(a)
    if sc<best: update; 
    if time... break
  return best_a,best

Let's define `active_gradient_refine(a0, deadline, n_iter=300, top_mode=0)`:
```
def active_gradient_refine(a0, deadline, n_iter=300):
    a=normalize_positive(a0); best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a)
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    it=0
    while it<n_iter and time.monotonic()<deadline:
       c=fast_conv(a)
       sc=score_from_c(a,c)
       if sc<best_sc: update
       cn=c/c.sum()
       if it%3==0:
          w=top_weights(cn, 20, 0.02)
       elif it%3==1:
          w=softmax_weights(cn, 5000)
       else:
          w=softmax_weights(cn, 200)
       _,g=conv_weight_grad(a,w,c)
       gm=np.mean(g); v=(g-gm); vn=np.sqrt(np.mean(v*v)); if vn<1e-30: break
       v=v/vn
       best_step=None; best_cand=a; best_cand_sc=sc
       # additive steps
       for s in [0.001,0.003,0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3]:
           cand=np.maximum(a - s*a.mean()*v, 0.0)
           cand=normalize_positive(cand)
           sc2=score(cand) # calls fast conv; maybe expensive
           if sc2<best_cand_sc: update
       if best_cand is not a: a=best_cand; 
       # multiplicative? maybe skip to save
       it+=1
    return best_a,bst_sc
```
Score calls 6 per iter *300=1800 FFTs plus conv_weight_grad 300 (two FFTs) -> maybe 60s. okay. But `score(cand)` calls fast_conv and sum; no grad. We can optimize by computing c for candidates? Need separate. fine.

def lbfgs_callback(res):
    if time.monotonic() >= GLOBAL_DEADLINE:
        raise OptTimeout()
```

`_next_power_of_two(L)`:
```
def next_power_of_two(L):
    if L <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()
```

`normalize_positive`:
```
def normalize_positive(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0: return np.ones(1)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-14:
       return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1,a.size)
    return a / s
```

`fast_conv`:
```
def fast_conv(a):
   n=int(len(a))
   if n==0: return np.empty(0)
   L=2*n-1
   M=next_power_of_two(L)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a, M)
   c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, M)[:L]
   # clean tiny imaginary? irfft real
   return c
```
Potential if a has nonfinite, rfft returns nan. Objective returns large if not finite.

`score_from_c`:
```
def score_from_c(a,c):
    n=len(a); S=float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S < 0.01: return float('inf')
    maxc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(maxc): return float('inf')
    if maxc < 0: maxc = 0.0
    return float(2.0*n*maxc/(S*S))
```

`score(a)` simple.

`conv_weight_grad`:
```
def conv_weight_grad(a, p, c=None):
   n=len(a)
   if c is None: c=fast_conv(a)
   L=2*n-1
   out_len=n+L-1
   M=next_power_of_two(out_len)
   ra=a[::-1]
   RA=np.fft.rfft(ra, M)
   P=np.fft.rfft(p, M)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(RA*P, M)
   g=2.0*conv[n-1:2*n-1]
   # Ensure length n
   if g.size != n:
      if g.size < n: g=np.concatenate([g, np.zeros(n-g.size)])
      else: g=g[:n]
   return c, g
```
For n=1: n-1=0, 2*n-1=1, ok.

`lse_value_weights(c,beta)` as above. Need if beta not finite or <=0.

`obj_lse(x,beta,beta_cap?)`: Use `PEN=1e-8`.
```
def obj_lse(x, beta):
    a=np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: return 1e100, np.array([])
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    S=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S<1e-8:
       GLOBAL_LAST_X=np.maximum(x,0.0).astype(np.float64)
       return 1e100, np.zeros(len(a))
    beta_eff=float(beta) / (S*S)
    if beta_eff <= 1e-12: beta_eff=1e-12
    if beta_eff > 1e12: beta_eff=1e12
    c=fast_conv(a)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
       GLOBAL_LAST_X=np.maximum(x,0.0).astype(np.float64)
       return 1e100, np.zeros(len(a))
    Lval,p=lse_value_weights(c,beta_eff)
    _,g=conv_weight_grad(a,p,c)
    n=len(a)
    score=(2.0*n*Lval)/(S*S)
    grad=(2.0*n)*(g*S - 2.0*Lval)/(S*S*S)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
       grad=np.zeros(len(a))
    # penalty
    pen=1e-8
    score += pen*(S-1.0)*(S-1.0)
    grad = grad + (2.0*pen*(S-1.0))*np.ones(n)
    GLOBAL_LAST_X = a.copy() # maybe use x clipped? use a
    return float(score), grad
```
But if we use `a` normalized? No, a clipped. `GLOBAL_LAST_X` as a (clipped). If S very large, objective penalty penalizes. Good. Is gradient formula with Lval raw LSE and S works? Yes for score=2n Lval/S^2. But Lval is LSE_βeff(c). The penalty gradient added. Good.

However, Lval raw maybe scale with S^2. If S=1, fine. If S not 1, beta_eff ensures Lval = S^2 L_q. Gradient C' from conv_weight_grad raw. Formula okay. But `g` raw derivative of Lval wrt a? For LSE_βeff(c) with βeff constant; derivative =∑p C'_k. C'_k raw derivative. p computed from c raw with βeff. Good.

`obj_weighted(x,w,Pfft,M2,n)`:

def obj_weighted(x,w,Pfft,M2,n):
   a=np.maximum(np.asarray(x,float),0)
   S=... if invalid...
   c=fast_conv(a)
   C=float(np.dot(w,c))
   if not finite C...

   conv=irfft(RA*Pfft,M2)
   g=2*conv[n-1:2*n-1]
   score=2*n*C/(S*S)
   grad=2*n*(g*S - 2*C)/(S*S*S)
   penalty add
   GLOBAL_LAST_X=a.copy()

```

`run_lbfgs`:
```
def run_lbfgs(a0, kind, params, maxfun=5000, maxiter=2000, deadline=0.0):
   a0=normalize_positive(a0)
   n=len(a0)
   if n<=0: return np.ones(1)
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   if kind=='lse':
       fun=obj_lse; args=(float(params),)
   elif kind=='weighted':
       w=np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64)
       L=2*n-1
       if w.size != L:
          # interpolate or uniform
          if w.size > L: w=np.interp(np.linspace(0,L-1,L), np.linspace(0,w.size-1,w.size), w)
          else: w=np.interp(np.linspace(0,L-1,L), np.linspace(0,w.size-1,w.size), w)
       w=np.maximum(w,0.0); ws=w.sum(); if ws<=0: w=np.ones(L)/L else w=w/ws
       M2=next_power_of_two(3*n-2)
       Pfft=np.fft.rfft(w, M2)
       fun=obj_weighted; args=(w,Pfft,M2,n)
   else: return a0
   GLOBAL_DEADLINE=deadline
   GLOBAL_LAST_X=a0.copy()
   options={'ftol':1e-16,'gtol':1e-12,'maxls':50,'maxiter':int(maxiter),'maxfun':int(maxfun)}
   try:
      res=minimize(fun, a0, args=args, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds, callback=lbfgs_callback, options=options)
      x=res.x
   except OptTimeout:
      x=GLOBAL_LAST_X
   except Exception as e:
      print('LBFGS exception:', e)
      x=a0
   if GLOBAL_LAST_X is not None and x is None: x=GLOBAL_LAST_X
   x=np.asarray(x, float); x=np.maximum(x,0)
   s=x.sum(); if s<1e-12: x=a0
   return x
```
Maybe `maxiter` should less than maxfun; set maxiter=max(maxfun//10,100). For n 7000. Use `options={'maxiter':2000, 'maxfun':5000, 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-12, 'maxls':40}`. If beta high, gtol may stop early. Good.

`make_filter_h(m, kind)`:
```
def make_filter_h(m, kind):
   if m < 1: m=1
   x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
   xm=x - 0.5
   if kind=='box': h=np.ones(m)
   elif kind=='tri': h=np.maximum(0.0,1.0-2.0*abs(xm))
   elif kind=='gauss': sigma=max(0.05,m/6.0); h=np.exp(-(xm*xm)/(2*sigma*sigma))
   elif kind=='center': h=0.5+0.5*np.cos(np.pi*x)  # zero at edges? cos pi*0=1 ->1 at x0, 0 at edge? x=1 cosπ=-1, h0; good center peak.
   elif kind=='edge': h=0.5+0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*x) # high at edges, low middle
   else: h=np.ones(m)
   h=np.maximum(h,1e-8); h=h/h.sum(); return h
```
`apply_filter(a,h)`:
```
def apply_filter(a,h):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); h=np.asarray(h,float)
   L=len(a)+len(h)-1
   M=next_power_of_two(L)
   A=np.fft.rfft(a,M); H=np.fft.rfft(h,M)
   b=np.fft.irfft(A*H,M)[:L]
   b=np.maximum(b,0)
   return b
```

```
def filter_objective(x, base, base_fft, M):
    h=np.asarray(x,float); m=len(h)
    h=np.maximum(h,0); hs=h.sum()
    if hs<1e-12: return 1e100
    h=h/hs
    H=np.fft.rfft(h,M)
    b=np.fft.irfft(base_fft*H,M)[:len(base)+m-1]
    b=np.maximum(b,0); S=b.sum(); if S<1e-12: return 1e100
    b=b/S
    c=fast_conv(b)
    return score_from_c(b,c)
```

           h=res.x
       except Exception:

`flat_target(n)` and `adaptive_target`. `soft target` may want quantile. Use `np.quantile` available.
```
def flat_target(n):
   L=2*n-1; return np.ones(L)/L
def adaptive_target(c, mix=0.5, quantile=0.99):
   L=len(c); cs=c.sum(); if cs<=0 or not finite: return flat
   cn=c/cs
   if quantile is None: cap=cn.max()
   else: cap=float(np.quantile(cn, quantile))
   clipped=np.minimum(cn, cap)
   csum=clipped.sum(); if csum<=0: clipped=cn
   else: clipped=clipped/csum
   t=(1-mix)*np.ones(L)/L + mix*clipped
   t=np.maximum(t,0); s=t.sum(); if s<=0: t=np.ones(L)/L else t=t/s
   return t
```

`poly_square_gs(a, target, n_iters, alpha_init, alpha_final)`:
```
def poly_square_gs(a, target, n_iters=100, alpha_init=0.5, alpha_final=0.02):
   a=normalize_positive(a); n=len(a); target=np.asarray(target,float)
   L=2*n-1; if target.size<L: pad; if >L: truncate
   t=target[:L]; t=t/t.sum() if sum>0 else flat
   M=next_power_of_two(L)
   A=np.fft.fft(a,M)
   T=np.fft.fft(t,M)
   best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a)
   stopiters=0
   for it in range(n_iters):
      alpha = alpha_init + (alpha_final-alpha_init)*it/max(1,n_iters-1)
      # if T nan? T finite
      A_des=np.sqrt(T)
      real_part=np.real(A_des * np.conj(A))
      flip=real_part < 0
      if np.any(flip): A_des=np.where(flip, -A_des, A_des)  # but np.where returns? A_des = A_des.copy? okay
      A = A + alpha*(A_des - A)
      a_new=np.fft.ifft(A,M).real[:n]
      a_new=np.maximum(a_new,0.0)
      S=a_new.sum(); if S<1e-12: break
      a_new=a_new/S
      c=fast_conv(a_new); sc=score_from_c(a_new,c)
      if sc < best_sc:
         best_sc=sc; best_a=a_new.copy()
         stopiters=0
      else:
         stopiters+=1
         # if long no improvement, reduce alpha? maybe break after 100
         if stopiters > 150 and it>50: break
   return best_a,best_sc
```
`A_des=np.where(flip, -A_des, A_des)`: if flip bool, -A_des, A_des. okay. Need if T has zeros; sqrt zero. Good.
Could use `T = T + 1e-30` to avoid zero? Not necessary.

But target t sum1, T at freq 0 =1. A at freq0 for normalized a =1. sqrt(1)=1, branch okay. Good.

Potential issue: If T has negative real/phase, sqrt branch. okay.

`poly_square_gs` may produce a with high nonnegativity? Clipping may distort strongly; best kept. fine.

`make_symmetric(a)`: `b=0.5*(a+a[::-1])`; normalize.

`reweight_loop` should not exceed deadline. It calls run_lbfgs with deadline; each run stops near deadline. Use `deadline` in each.

Let's define `reweight_loop(a0, deadline, rng)`:
```
def reweight_loop(a0, deadline, rng):
   a=normalize...; best...
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1
   Ks=[1,2,5,10,25,60,150,300]
   betas=[100,500,2000,10000,50000]
   mixs=[0.0,0.01,0.03]
   it=0
   while time<deadline-30:
      # choose mode random-ish using rng integer
      mode=int(rng.integers(0,5))
      c=fast_conv(a); sc=score...
      cn=c/c.sum() if c.sum()>0 else np.ones(L)/L
      if mode<2:
         K=Ks[int(rng.integers(0,len(Ks)))]
         w=make_top_weights(cn,K,mix=float(rng.random()*0.05))
      elif mode<4:
         beta=betas[int(rng.integers(0,len(betas)))]
         w=softmax_weights(cn,beta)
         w=float(rng.random()*0.1)*w + (1-...)*np.ones(L)/L # Actually add uniform
      else: # combine top and softmax
         K=...; beta=...
         w1=make_top...; w2=softmax; w=0.5/w normalize
      w = w/w.sum()
      mf=4000 if n<8000 else 2000
      a=run_lbfgs(a,'weighted',w,mf,max(500,mf//2), deadline-20)
      a=normalize...
      # if no improvement many? could continue
      it+=1
      if it%8==0:
         # apply short GS adaptive to escape
         if time < deadline-60:
            target=adaptive_target(fast_conv(a), mix=float(rng.uniform(0.3,0.8)), quantile=float(rng.uniform(0.9,0.999)))
            a_gs,sc=poly_square_gs(a,target,60,0.3,0.05)
            # use as next a even if score worse? Maybe if not improve? Use if score(a_gs) < score(a) or rng<0.3
            sc_gs=score(a_gs)
            if sc_gs < score(a) or rng.random()<0.3:
                a=normalize_positive(a_gs)
            # update best
   return best_a,best_sc
```
Need update best inside. Use `score` each loop. To avoid too many score calls, compute c and score once. When updating a from GS, compute score.

`softmax_weights(cn, beta)`:
```
def softmax_weights(cn,beta):
   if beta <=0: return ones/L
   L=len(cn); cmax=cn.max(); z=beta*(cn-cmax); ez=np.exp(z); s=ez.sum(); if s<=0: onehot; return ez/s
```
Use normalized cn.

`make_top_weights(cn, K, mix)`:
```
def make_top_weights(cn, K, mix=0.05):
   L=len(cn); w=np.zeros(L)
   K=int(min(K,L))
   if K >0:
      if K==L: w=np.ones(L)/L
      else:
         idx=np.argpartition(cn, -K)[-K:]
         w[idx]=1.0/K
   if mix>0:
      w=(1-mix)*w + mix*(1.0/L)
   s=w.sum(); if s<=0: w=np.ones(L)/L else w=w/s
   return w
```
`np.argpartition` for K maybe 1: works? `argpartition(cn, -1)` returns last max? For K=1, use `np.argmax`. Implement safe:
```
if K==1: idx=np.array([np.argmax(cn)])
elif K>=L: all
else: idx=np.argpartition(cn, -K)[-K:]
```

Now, `active_gradient_refine` details:
```
def active_gradient_refine(a0, deadline, n_iter=200):
  a=normalize; best...
  n=len(a); L=2n-1
  top_betas=[50,500,5000,20000]
  for it in range(n_iter):
    if time>=deadline: break
    c=fast_conv(a); sc=score...
    cn=c/c.sum()
    # w
    if it%4==0: w=make_top_weights(cn, 20, 0.02)
    elif it%4==1: w=softmax_weights(cn, 200)
    elif it%4==2: w=softmax_weights(cn, 5000)
    else: w=softmax_weights(cn, 50000)
    _,g=conv_weight_grad(a,w,c)
    gmean=float(np.mean(g))
    v=g-gmean
    var=float(np.var(v))
    if var<1e-30: break
    v=v/np.sqrt(var)
    base=a.mean()
    best_cand=a; best_cand_sc=sc
    for s in [0.001,0.003,0.01,0.03]:
       cand=np.maximum(a - s*base*v,0)
       cand=normalize_positive(cand)
       sc2=score(cand)
       if sc2<best_cand_sc: best_cand=cand; best_cand_sc=sc2
    # multiplicative maybe one
    for eta in [0.005,0.02,0.08]:
       cand=np.maximum(a * np.exp(-eta*v),0)
       cand=normalize_positive(cand)
       sc2=score(cand)
       if sc2<best_cand_sc: best_cand=cand; best_cand_sc=sc2
    if best_cand_sc < sc:
       a=best_cand; update best
    else:
       # try small random? Could break if many no improve
  return best_a,best_sc
```
This is expensive: 7 score calls per iter. 200 ->1400 evals plus grad 200. Maybe okay if final. Limit n_iter=120, steps [0.002,0.01,0.03], multiplicative [0.01,0.05] =>5 evals/iter=600. Good.

Use `score(cand)` calls fast_conv; no grad. OK.

`make_initial_seed` code:
```
def make_initial_seed(n, rng):
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    target=flat_target(n)
    candidates=[]
    candidates.append(np.ones(n))
    candidates.append(np.maximum(0.01, 0.5 + 0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)))
    candidates.append(np.maximum(0.01, 0.5 - 0.5*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)))
    candidates.append(np.exp(-6.0*(x-0.5)*(x-0.5)))
    candidates.append((0.5+0.5*np.cos(np.pi*x))**2)
    # random smooth: r=rng.normal(size=n); h=make_filter_h(31,'gauss'); b=apply_filter(r,h) maybe can be negative; use abs
    r=rng.normal(size=n); r=r-r.min()+1.0; candidates.append(r)
    best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf
    for c in candidates:
       c=normalize_positive(c)
       # small GS
       if n > 4096: itu=40 else 80
       a2, sc = poly_square_gs(c, target, itu, 0.6, 0.2)
       if sc < best_sc: best_a=a2; best_sc=sc
       csc=score(c)
       if csc<best_sc: best_a=c.copy(); best_sc=csc
    return best_a, best_sc
```
For random normal, not smooth enough? Could convolve with gauss:
```
r=rng.normal(size=n); h=make_filter_h(33,'gauss'); r=apply_filter(r,h); r=np.maximum(r,1e-3); candidates.append(r)
```
Good.

   a = np.where(a>1e-4, a, 0.0)? keep zeros? normalize
   no_improve=0
```

def lse_sweep(a0, deadline, betas=None, maxfun=5000):
    if betas is None: betas=(50,200,1000,5000,25000,100000,400000)
    a=normalize_positive(a0)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score(a)
    for beta in betas:
       if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
       mf=maxfun
       if beta >= 25000: mf=max(1500, maxfun//3)
       elif beta >= 5000: mf=max(2000, maxfun//2)

       a=normalize_positive(a_new)
       sc=score(a)
       if sc < best_sc:
           best_sc=sc; best_a=a.copy()
    return best_a,best_sc

```python
"""
Search for a nonnegative coefficient sequence minimizing
2*n*max(conv(a,a))/(sum(a)^2).

Method:
- Use provided parent sequence if available, otherwise build seeds.
- FFT-based exact scoring and gradients.
- LSE-smoothed L-BFGS with annealed temperature.
- Active-set/reweighted quadratic L-BFGS phases.
- Polynomial square-root GS (solve a^2 ~= target coefficient sequence).
- Low-dimensional positive filter optimization to reshape/lengthen the best sequence.
- Final exact subgradient-style polishing and direct verification.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

GLOBAL_DEADLINE = 0.0
GLOBAL_LAST_X = None


class OptTimeout(Exception):
    pass


def lbfgs_callback(res):
    if time.monotonic() >= GLOBAL_DEADLINE:
        raise OptTimeout()


def next_power_of_two(L):
    L = int(L)
    if L <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_positive(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-14:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, a.size)
    return a / s


def fast_conv(a):
    n = int(len(a))
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    M = next_power_of_two(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, M)[:L]
    return c


def score_from_c(a, c):
    n = int(len(a))
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S < 0.01:
        return float("inf")
    if c.size == 0:
        maxc = 0.0
    else:
        maxc = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(maxc):
            return float("inf")
        if maxc < 0.0:
            maxc = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * maxc / (S * S))


def score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    c = fast_conv(a)
    return score_from_c(a, c)


def conv_weight_grad(a, p, c=None):
    n = int(len(a))
    if c is None:
        c = fast_conv(a)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    out_len = n + L - 1
    M = next_power_of_two(out_len)
    ra = a[::-1]
    RA = np.fft.rfft(ra, M)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, M)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(RA * P, M)
    seg = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    if seg.size < n:
        seg = np.concatenate([seg, np.zeros(n - seg.size, dtype=np.float64)])
    elif seg.size > n:
        seg = seg[:n]
    g = 2.0 * seg
    g[~np.isfinite(g)] = 0.0
    return c, g


def softmax_weights(cn, beta):
    cn = np.asarray(cn, dtype=np.float64)
    L = int(cn.size)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.ones(1)
    cn = np.where(np.isfinite(cn), cn, 0.0)
    s = float(cn.sum())
    if s <= 1e-30:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L
    cn = cn / s
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 1e-12:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L
    cmax = float(np.max(cn))
    z = beta * (cn - cmax)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    zsum = float(ez.sum())
    if zsum <= 1e-30 or not np.isfinite(zsum):
        idx = int(np.argmax(cn))
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0
        return w
    return ez / zsum


def make_top_weights(cn, K, mix=0.05):
    cn = np.asarray(cn, dtype=np.float64)
    L = int(cn.size)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.ones(1)
    cn = np.where(np.isfinite(cn), cn, 0.0)
    s = float(cn.sum())
    if s <= 1e-30:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L
    cn = cn / s
    K = int(max(1, min(K, L)))
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    if K == L:
        w[:] = 1.0 / L
    elif K == 1:
        w[int(np.argmax(cn))] = 1.0
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(cn, -K)[-K:]
        w[idx] = 1.0 / K
    mix = float(mix)
    if mix > 0.0:
        w = (1.0 - mix) * w + mix * (1.0 / L)
    ws = float(w.sum())
    if ws <= 1e-30 or not np.isfinite(ws):
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L
    return w / ws


def lse_value_weights(c, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = int(c.size)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        bad = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        return cmax, bad
    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 1e-12:
        mean = float(np.mean(c)) if L else 0.0
        p = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, L)
        return mean, p
    z = beta * (c - cmax)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    zsum = float(ez.sum())
    if zsum <= 1e-30 or not np.isfinite(zsum):
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return cmax, p
    p = ez / zsum
    Lval = cmax + float(np.log(zsum)) / beta
    return Lval, p


def update_from_score(best_score, best_a, sc, cand):
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return best_score, best_a
    cand = normalize_positive(cand)
    if float(sc) < float(best_score):
        return float(sc), cand.copy()
    return best_score, best_a


def obj_lse(x, beta):
    global GLOBAL_LAST_X
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return 1e100, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S < 1e-10:
        GLOBAL_LAST_X = a.copy()
        return 1e100, np.zeros(a.shape, dtype=np.float64)

    beta_eff = float(beta) / (S * S)
    if not np.isfinite(beta_eff) or beta_eff <= 1e-12:
        beta_eff = 1e-12
    if beta_eff > 1e12:
        beta_eff = 1e12

    c = fast_conv(a)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        GLOBAL_LAST_X = a.copy()
        return 1e100, np.zeros(a.shape, dtype=np.float64)

    Lval, p = lse_value_weights(c, beta_eff)
    if not np.isfinite(Lval):
        GLOBAL_LAST_X = a.copy()
        return 1e100, np.zeros(a.shape, dtype=np.float64)

    _, g = conv_weight_grad(a, p, c)
    n = int(a.size)
    S2 = S * S
    S3 = S2 * S
    sc = (2.0 * n * Lval) / S2
    grad = (2.0 * n) * (g * S - 2.0 * Lval) / S3

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    mu = 1e-8
    sc += mu * (S - 1.0) * (S - 1.0)
    grad = grad + (2.0 * mu * (S - 1.0))
    GLOBAL_LAST_X = a.copy()
    return float(sc), grad


def obj_weighted(x, w, Pfft, M2, n):
    global GLOBAL_LAST_X
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = int(n)
    if a.size != n:
        a = np.resize(a, n)
    if a.size == 0:
        return 1e100, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S < 1e-10:
        GLOBAL_LAST_X = a.copy()
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    c = fast_conv(a)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        GLOBAL_LAST_X = a.copy()
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    C = float(np.dot(w, c))
    if not np.isfinite(C):
        GLOBAL_LAST_X = a.copy()
        return 1e100, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    RA = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], M2)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(RA * Pfft, M2)
    seg = conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    if seg.size < n:
        seg = np.concatenate([seg, np.zeros(n - seg.size, dtype=np.float64)])
    elif seg.size > n:
        seg = seg[:n]
    g = 2.0 * seg
    g[~np.isfinite(g)] = 0.0

    S2 = S * S
    S3 = S2 * S
    sc = (2.0 * n * C) / S2
    grad = (2.0 * n) * (g * S - 2.0 * C) / S3

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    mu = 1e-8
    sc += mu * (S - 1.0) * (S - 1.0)
    grad = grad + (2.0 * mu * (S - 1.0))
    GLOBAL_LAST_X = a.copy()
    return float(sc), grad


def run_lbfgs(a0, kind, params, maxfun=5000, maxiter=1500, deadline=0.0):
    global GLOBAL_DEADLINE, GLOBAL_LAST_X
    a0 = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = int(a0.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    if kind == "lse":
        fun = obj_lse
        args = (float(params),)
    elif kind == "weighted":
        w = np.asarray(params, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        L = 2 * n - 1
        if w.size != L:
            if w.size == 0:
                w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L
            else:
                old_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, w.size)
                new_x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, L)
                w = np.interp(new_x, old_x, w)
        w[~np.isfinite(w)] = 0.0
        w = np.maximum(w, 0.0)
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if ws <= 1e-30:
            w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L
        else:
            w = w / ws
        M2 = next_power_of_two(3 * n - 2)
        Pfft = np.fft.rfft(w, M2)
        fun = obj_weighted
        args = (w, Pfft, M2, n)
    else:
        return a0.copy()

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    maxfun = int(max(100, maxfun))
    maxiter = int(max(50, min(maxiter, max(50, maxfun // 2))))

    GLOBAL_DEADLINE = float(deadline)
    GLOBAL_LAST_X = a0.copy()

    options = {
        "ftol": 1e-16,
        "gtol": 1e-12,
        "maxls": 50,
        "maxiter": maxiter,
        "maxfun": maxfun,
    }

    try:
        res = minimize(
            fun,
            a0,
            args=args,
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            bounds=bounds,
            callback=lbfgs_callback,
            options=options,
        )
        x = res.x
    except OptTimeout:
        x = GLOBAL_LAST_X
    except Exception as e:
        print("LBFGS exception:", repr(e))
        x = a0

    if x is None:
        x = GLOBAL_LAST_X if GLOBAL_LAST_X is not None else a0
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    x[~np.isfinite(x)] = 0.0
    x = np.maximum(x, 0.0)
    s = float(x.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        x = a0.copy()
    return x


def make_filter_h(m, kind):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    if kind == "box":
        h = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    elif kind == "tri":
        xm = x - 0.5
        h = np.maximum(0.0, 1.0 - 2.0 * np.abs(xm))
    elif kind == "gauss":
        xm = x - 0.5
        sig = max(0.08, m / 8.0)
        h = np.exp(-(xm * xm) / (2.0 * sig * sig))
    elif kind == "center":
        h = np.cos(np.pi * (x - 0.5)) ** 2
    elif kind == "edge":
        h = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    else:
        h = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    h = np.maximum(h, 1e-9)
    s = float(h.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        h = np.ones(m, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(m)
    return h / s


def apply_filter(a, h):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
    L = int(a.size) + int(h.size) - 1
    M = next_power_of_two(L)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, M)
    H = np.fft.rfft(h, M)
    b = np.fft.irfft(A * H, M)[:L]
    b[~np.isfinite(b)] = 0.0
    return np.maximum(b, 0.0)


def filter_objective(x, base, base_fft, M):
    h = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    m = int(h.size)
    if m <= 0:
        return 1e100
    h[~np.isfinite(h)] = 0.0
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    hs = float(h.sum())
    if hs < 1e-14:
        return 1e100
    h = h / hs
    H = np.fft.rfft(h, M)
    L = int(base.size) + m - 1
    b = np.fft.irfft(base_fft * H, M)[:L]
    b[~np.isfinite(b)] = 0.0
    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    S = float(b.sum())
    if S < 1e-14:
        return 1e100
    b = b / S
    c = fast_conv(b)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
        return 1e100
    return score_from_c(b, c)


def optimize_filter(base, m, kinds, deadline, maxiter=500):
    base = normalize_positive(base)
    M = next_power_of_two(int(base.size) + int(m) - 1)
    base_fft = np.fft.rfft(base, M)
    best_a = base.copy()
    best_sc = score(base)

    for kind in kinds:
        if time.monotonic() >= float(deadline):
            break
        h0 = make_filter_h(m, kind)
        try:
            res = minimize(
                filter_objective,
                h0,
                args=(base, base_fft, M),
                method="Nelder-Mead",
                options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "xatol": 1e-10, "fatol": 1e-13},
            )
            h = res.x
        except Exception as e:
            print("filter optimization exception:", repr(e))
            h = h0
        h = normalize_positive(h)
        b = apply_filter(base, h)
        sc = score(b)
        best_sc, best_a = update_from_score(best_sc, best_a, sc, b)
    return best_a, best_sc


def flat_target(n):
    L = 2 * int(n) - 1
    return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L


def adaptive_target(c, mix=0.5, quantile=0.99):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = int(c.size)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.ones(1)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    cs = float(c.sum())
    if cs <= 1e-30:
        return np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L
    cn = c / cs
    if quantile is None:
        cap = float(np.max(cn))
    else:
        q = float(min(max(quantile, 0.0), 1.0))
        cap = float(np.quantile(cn, q))
    clipped = np.minimum(cn, cap)
    csum = float(clipped.sum())
    if csum <= 1e-30:
        clipped = cn
    else:
        clipped = clipped / csum
    flat = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L
    mix = float(min(max(mix, 0.0), 1.0))
    t = (1.0 - mix) * flat + mix * clipped
    t = np.where(np.isfinite(t), t, 0.0)
    t = np.maximum(t, 0.0)
    ts = float(t.sum())
    if ts <= 1e-30:
        t = flat
    else:
        t = t / ts
    return t


def poly_square_gs(a, target, n_iters=100, alpha_init=0.50, alpha_final=0.03):
    a = normalize_positive(a)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    target = np.asarray(target, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if target.size < L:
        target = np.pad(target, (0, L - target.size), mode="constant")
    elif target.size > L:
        target = target[:L]
    target = np.where(np.isfinite(target), target, 0.0)
    target = np.maximum(target, 0.0)
    ts = float(target.sum())
    if ts <= 1e-30:
        target = flat_target(n)
    else:
        target = target / ts

    M = next_power_of_two(L)
    A = np.fft.fft(a, M)
    T = np.fft.fft(target, M)

    best_a = a.copy()
    c0 = fast_conv(a)
    best_sc = score_from_c(a, c0)

    no_improve = 0
    n_iters = int(max(1, n_iters))
    for it in range(n_iters):
        alpha = alpha_init + (alpha_final - alpha_init) * float(it) / float(n_iters - 1 if n_iters > 1 else 1)
        A_des = np.sqrt(T)
        choice = np.real(A_des * np.conj(A)) < 0.0
        if np.any(choice):
            A_des = np.where(choice, -A_des, A_des)
        A = A + alpha * (A_des - A)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(A)):
            break
        a_new = np.fft.ifft(A, M).real[:n]
        a_new[~np.isfinite(a_new)] = 0.0
        a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
        S = float(a_new.sum())
        if S <= 1e-14:
            break
        a_new = a_new / S
        c = fast_conv(a_new)
        sc = score_from_c(a_new, c)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 160 and it > 60:
                break
    return best_a, best_sc


def interp_to_len(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b[~np.isfinite(b)] = 0.0
    return np.maximum(b, 0.0)


def make_initial_seed(n, rng):
    n = int(max(16, n))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    target = flat_target(n)
    candidates = []
    candidates.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    candidates.append(np.maximum(1e-3, 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)))
    candidates.append(np.maximum(1e-3, 0.5 - 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)))
    candidates.append(np.exp(-6.0 * (x - 0.5) * (x - 0.5)))
    candidates.append((0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * x)) ** 2)
    r = rng.normal(size=n)
    r = apply_filter(r, make_filter_h(33, "gauss"))
    r = np.maximum(r, 1e-3)
    candidates.append(r)

    best_a = None
    best_sc = float("inf")
    for c in candidates:
        c = normalize_positive(c)
        sc = score(c)
        best_sc, best_a = update_from_score(best_sc, best_a, sc, c)
        iters = 45 if n > 5000 else 100
        a2, sc2 = poly_square_gs(c, target, iters, 0.55, 0.18)
        best_sc, best_a = update_from_score(best_sc, best_a, sc2, a2)

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        best_sc = 2.0
    return normalize_positive(best_a), float(best_sc)


def lse_sweep(a0, deadline, betas=None, maxfun=5000):
    if betas is None:
        betas = (50.0, 200.0, 1000.0, 5000.0, 25000.0, 100000.0, 400000.0)
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    c0 = fast_conv(a)
    best_sc = score_from_c(a, c0)
    best_a = a.copy()

    for beta in betas:
        if time.monotonic() >= float(deadline):
            break
        beta = float(beta)
        mf = int(maxfun)
        if beta >= 25000.0:
            mf = max(1200, int(maxfun // 4))
        elif beta >= 5000.0:
            mf = max(1800, int(maxfun // 2))
        mi = max(100, mf // 3)
        a_new = run_lbfgs(a, "lse", beta, maxfun=mf, maxiter=mi, deadline=float(deadline))
        a = normalize_positive(a_new)
        sc = score(a)
        best_sc, best_a = update_from_score(best_sc, best_a, sc, a)
    return best_a, best_sc


def reweight_loop(a0, deadline, rng):
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    c0 = fast_conv(a)
    best_sc = score_from_c(a, c0)
    best_a = a.copy()

    Ks = (1, 2, 5, 10, 25, 60, 150, 300)
    betas = (50.0, 500.0, 3000.0, 20000.0, 150000.0)
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 25.0:
        c = fast_conv(a)
        sc = score_from_c(a, c)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        cn = c / float(c.sum()) if float(c.sum()) > 1e-30 else np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L

        mode = int(rng.integers(0, 6))
        if no_improve >= 8 and mode < 2:
            # perturb to escape
            noise_scale = 0.004 + 0.01 * float(no_improve) / 20.0
            a = a + noise_scale * rng.standard_normal(n)
            a = normalize_positive(a)
            no_improve = 0
            continue

        if mode < 2:
            K = int(Ks[int(rng.integers(0, len(Ks)))])
            mix = float(rng.random() * 0.05)
            w = make_top_weights(cn, K, mix)
        elif mode < 4:
            beta = float(betas[int(rng.integers(0, len(betas)))])
            w = softmax_weights(cn, beta)
            umix = float(rng.random() * 0.05)
            w = (1.0 - umix) * w + umix * (1.0 / L)
        elif mode == 4:
            K = int(Ks[int(rng.integers(0, len(Ks)))])
            w1 = make_top_weights(cn, K, 0.02)
            beta = float(betas[int(rng.integers(0, len(betas)))])
            w2 = softmax_weights(cn, beta)
            w = 0.5 * w1 + 0.5 * w2
        else:
            K = max(8, int(Ks.min()))
            w = make_top_weights(cn, K, 0.10)

        ws = float(w.sum())
        if ws <= 1e-30:
            w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L
        else:
            w = w / ws

        mf = 3500 if n <= 8000 else 1800
        mi = max(120, mf // 3)
        a2 = run_lbfgs(a, "weighted", w, maxfun=mf, maxiter=mi, deadline=float(deadline) - 15.0)
        a = normalize_positive(a2)

        if it % 7 == 6:
            try:
                if time.monotonic() < float(deadline) - 80.0:
                    c3 = fast_conv(a)
                    sc3 = score_from_c(a, c3)
                    if sc3 > best_sc and rng.random() < 0.8:
                        continue
                    mix = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.85))
                    q = float(rng.uniform(0.90, 0.999))
                    tgt = adaptive_target(c3, mix=mix, quantile=q)
                    a_gs, sc_gs = poly_square_gs(a, tgt, 45, 0.35, 0.06)
                    sc_gs2 = score(a_gs)
                    best_sc2, best_a2 = update_from_score(best_sc, best_a, sc_gs2, a_gs)
                    best_sc, best_a = best_sc2, best_a2
                    if sc_gs2 < sc3 or rng.random() < 0.25:
                        a = normalize_positive(a_gs)
            except Exception as e:
                print("reweight GS exception:", repr(e))

        it += 1
        if it % 10 == 0:
            print(f"    reweight it={it}: best={best_sc:.10f}")

    return best_a, best_sc


def active_gradient_refine(a0, deadline, n_iter=120):
    a = normalize_positive(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    c0 = fast_conv(a)
    best_sc = score_from_c(a, c0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    L = 2 * n - 1

    bases = [50.0, 500.0, 5000.0, 50000.0]

    for it in range(int(n_iter)):
        if time.monotonic() >= float(deadline):
            break
        c = fast_conv(a)
        sc = score_from_c(a, c)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = a.copy()

        cn = c / float(c.sum()) if float(c.sum()) > 1e-30 else np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / L
        mode = it % 4
        if mode == 0:
            w = make_top_weights(cn, 24, 0.02)
        else:
            w = softmax_weights(cn, bases[mode - 1])

        _, g = conv_weight_grad(a, w, c)
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
        gm = float(np.mean(g))
        v = g - gm
        var = float(np.var(v))
        if var <= 1e-30:
            break
        v = v / np.sqrt(var)

        scale = float(np.std(a))
        if scale <= 1e-15:
            scale = max(1e-15, float(a.mean()))

        best_cand = a
        best_cand_sc = sc

        # additive line search
        for stp in (0.02, 0.10, 0.50, 2.00):
            cand = a - (stp * scale) * v
            cand = normalize_positive(cand)
            sc2 = score(cand)
            if sc2 < best_cand_sc:
                best_cand_sc = sc2
                best_cand = cand

        # multiplicative line search
        for eta in (0.05, 0.25, 1.00):
            cand = a * np.exp(-eta * v)
            cand = normalize_positive(cand)
            sc2 = score(cand)
            if sc2 < best_cand_sc:
                best_cand_sc = sc2
                best_cand = cand

        if best_cand_sc < sc:
            a = normalize_positive(best_cand)

    return best_a, best_sc


def to_valid_list(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01 or a.size == 0:
        return [0.5]
    return [float(v) for v in a]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(max(300.0, budget_s))
    opt_deadline = t_start + max(120.0, budget - 120.0)
    final_deadline = t_start + max(60.0, budget - 20.0)

    # Load parent if present.
    parent = None
    try:
        if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
            p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
                parent = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None and parent.size >= 64:
        if parent.size > 10000:
            n = 8000
            a = interp_to_len(parent, n)
        else:
            a = parent.copy()
    else:
        n = 6962
        a, _ = make_initial_seed(n, rng)

    a = normalize_positive(a)
    c_initial = fast_conv(a)
    best_sc = score_from_c(a, c_initial)
    best_a = a.copy()
    print(f"initial: n={len(best_a)} score={best_sc:.10f}")

    total_opt = max(60.0, opt_deadline - t_start)
    end1 = t_start + 0.40 * total_opt
    end2 = t_start + 0.75 * total_opt
    end3 = t_start + 0.90 * total_opt

    # Quick sanity/short smoothing transforms.
    quick_end = min(opt_deadline, t_start + 90.0)
    sym = 0.5 * (best_a + best_a[::-1])
    sc = score(sym)
    best_sc, best_a = update_from_score(best_sc, best_a, sc, sym)
    if best_sc < 2.0:
        print(f"sym improved: {best_sc:.10f}")

    for m in (3, 5, 9, 17, 31):
        if time.monotonic() > quick_end:
            break
        for kind in ("box", "tri", "gauss", "center", "edge"):
            if time.monotonic() > quick_end:
                break
            b = apply_filter(best_a, make_filter_h(m, kind))
            sc = score(b)
            best_sc, best_a = update_from_score(best_sc, best_a, sc, b)
    print(f"after quick transforms: {best_sc:.10f} n={len(best_a)}")

    # Main LSE sweep.
    dl1 = min(end1, opt_deadline)
    if time.monotonic() < dl1 - 30.0:
        a_lse, sc_lse = lse_sweep(best_a, dl1, betas=(50.0, 200.0, 1000.0, 5000.0, 25000.0, 100000.0), maxfun=5500)
        best_sc, best_a = update_from_score(best_sc, best_a, sc_lse, a_lse)
        print(f"after LSE sweep: {best_sc:.10f} n={len(best_a)}")

    # Polynomial square-root GS with adaptive target.
    if time.monotonic() < end2 - 120.0:
        c_now = fast_conv(best_a)
        target = adaptive_target(c_now, mix=0.55, quantile=0.99)
        a_gs, sc_gs = poly_square_gs(best_a, target, 220, 0.42, 0.025)
        sc_gs2 = score(a_gs)
        best_sc, best_a = update_from_score(best_sc, best_a, sc_gs2, a_gs)
        if best_sc < 2.0:
            print(f"after poly GS adaptive: {best_sc:.10f}")

        # A short LSE polish after GS.
        if time.monotonic() < end2 - 90.0:
            a_lse2, sc_lse2 = lse_sweep(best_a, min(end2 - 30.0, opt_deadline), betas=(10000.0, 80000.0), maxfun=3500)
            best_sc, best_a = update_from_score(best_sc, best_a, sc_lse2, a_lse2)

    # Active-set/reweighted phase.
    dl2 = min(end2, opt_deadline)
    if time.monotonic() < dl2 - 60.0:
        a_rw, sc_rw = reweight_loop(best_a, dl2, rng)
        best_sc, best_a = update_from_score(best_sc, best_a, sc_rw, a_rw)
        print(f"after reweight loop: {best_sc:.10f} n={len(best_a)}")

    # Low-dimensional positive filter optimization.
    dl3 = min(end3, opt_deadline)
    if time.monotonic() < dl3 - 120.0:
        for m in (9, 15, 31):
            if time.monotonic() > dl3:
                break
            a_f, sc_f = optimize_filter(best_a, m, ("gauss", "tri", "center", "edge"), min(dl3, opt_deadline) - 20.0, maxiter=500)
            best_sc, best_a = update_from_score(best_sc, best_a, sc_f, a_f)
            if time.monotonic() < dl3 - 80.0:
                a_fl, sc_fl = lse_sweep(best_a, min(final_deadline, opt_deadline) - 30.0, betas=(5000.0, 50000.0), maxfun=2500)
                best_sc, best_a = update_from_score(best_sc, best_a, sc_fl, a_fl)
        print(f"after filter phase: {best_sc:.10f} n={len(best_a)}")

    # Random perturbation escapes with high-beta LSE.
    if time.monotonic() < opt_deadline - 120.0:
        n_cur = int(best_a.size)
        for strength in (0.002, 0.006, 0.015, 0.030):
            if time.monotonic() > opt_deadline - 70.0:
                break
            pert = best_a + strength * rng.standard_normal(n_cur)
            pert = normalize_positive(pert)
            beta = 200000.0 if strength < 0.01 else 50000.0
            a_p, sc_p = lse_sweep(pert, min(opt_deadline - 10.0, final_deadline - 30.0), betas=(beta,), maxfun=2500)
            best_sc, best_a = update_from_score(best_sc, best_a, sc_p, a_p)

    # Exact-ish active polishing.
    if time.monotonic() < opt_deadline - 40.0:
        a_act, sc_act = active_gradient_refine(best_a, min(opt_deadline, final_deadline - 30.0), n_iter=120)
        best_sc, best_a = update_from_score(best_sc, best_a, sc_act, a_act)
        print(f"after active polish: {best_sc:.10f} n={len(best_a)}")

    # Final symmetry check.
    sym2 = 0.5 * (best_a + best_a[::-1])
    sc2 = score(sym2)
    best_sc, best_a = update_from_score(best_sc, best_a, sc2, sym2)

    # Final verification and optional last Rettig attempts.
    target = 1.5027
    for check in range(4):
        out = to_valid_list(best_a)
        if "evaluate_sequence" in globals():
            try:
                v = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
            except Exception as e:
                print("evaluate_sequence exception:", repr(e))
                v = best_sc
        else:
            v = score(np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64))

        if np.isfinite(v) and v < best_sc:
            best_sc = v
            best_a = normalize_positive(np.asarray(out, dtype=np.float64))

        print(f"verify {check}: internal={best_sc:.10f} verified={v:.10f} len={len(out)} t={time.monotonic()-t_start:.1f}s")
        if np.isfinite(v) and v <= target:
            break
        if time.monotonic() > final_deadline - 45.0:
            break

        # If still above target, use remaining time for a few additional aggressive steps.
        rem = final_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 60.0:
            break
        dl = min(final_deadline - 20.0, time.monotonic() + max(35.0, 0.45 * rem))
        a_x, sc_x = lse_sweep(best_a, dl, betas=(300000.0, 1000000.0, 5000000.0), maxfun=2500)
        best_sc, best_a = update_from_score(best_sc, best_a, sc_x, a_x)

        if time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 35.0:
            a_y, sc_y = active_gradient_refine(best_a, min(final_deadline - 20.0, time.monotonic() + 45.0), n_iter=60)
            best_sc, best_a = update_from_score(best_sc, best_a, sc_y, a_y)

    out = to_valid_list(best_a)

    # Safety fallback.
    if len(out) < 1 or sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [1.0]

    return out
```
